// ignore_for_file: avoid_print
//
// Real-network integration run. Hits a live public API
// (jsonplaceholder.typicode.com) — needs network; expect it to fail offline.
//
//   dart run example/real_run.dart
//
// Exercises: Dioman.install wiring, DiomanCache hit + LRU, DiomanShare dedup,
// DiomanAuth proactive refresh (opt-in via expiresAt), handle.dispose(), AND
// the per-request DiomanXxxOptions override for repath/cache/share/key/retry/
// log/auth.

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:dioman/dioman.dart';

const _base = 'https://jsonplaceholder.typicode.com';

/// Minimal token store whose access token can be swapped by onRefresh.
class _TM implements DiomanTokenManager {
  _TM(this._access);
  String? _access;
  @override
  String? get accessToken => _access;
  @override
  String? get refreshToken => 'refresh';
  @override
  void setTokens({required String accessToken, String? refreshToken}) =>
      _access = accessToken;
  @override
  void clear() => _access = null;
  void set(String? t) => _access = t;
}

/// Minimal in-memory [DiomanCachePersist] for this script.
class _MemCachePersist implements DiomanCachePersist {
  final _store = <String, dynamic>{};
  @override
  dynamic read(String key) => _store[key];
  @override
  Future<void> write(String key, Map<String, dynamic> value) async =>
      _store[key] = value;
  @override
  Future<void> remove(String key) async => _store.remove(key);
  @override
  Future<void> erase() async => _store.clear();
}

/// Counts requests that reach the END of the request chain = real outbound
/// calls. Cache/share/mock short-circuit earlier in the chain, so those never
/// increment this — making it a truthful network-hit counter.
class _NetCounter extends Interceptor {
  int count = 0;
  @override
  void onRequest(RequestOptions o, RequestInterceptorHandler h) {
    count++;
    h.next(o);
  }
}

/// Fails the run with a clear message instead of limping on with a wrong
/// exit code — every assertion below is meant to be load-bearing evidence,
/// not a printed "expect N" that nobody checks.
void _check(bool ok, String label) {
  print('  ${ok ? 'OK ' : 'FAIL'} — $label');
  if (!ok) throw StateError('assertion failed: $label');
}

Future<void> main() async {
  final tm = _TM('old');
  var refreshCount = 0;
  final net = _NetCounter();

  final dio = Dio(BaseOptions(
    baseUrl: _base,
    connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
    receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
  ));

  final api = Dioman(dio: dio, plugins: [
    const DiomanRepath(),
    DiomanCache(persist: _MemCachePersist()),
    const DiomanShare(policy: DiomanSharePolicy.start),
    DiomanLoading(onChanged: (loading) => print('loading=$loading')),
    DiomanAuth(
        tokenManager: tm,
        expiresAt: (token) => token == 'new'
            ? DateTime.now().add(const Duration(hours: 1))
            : DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(seconds: 1)),
        onRefresh: (tokens, _) {
          refreshCount++;
          tokens.setTokens(accessToken: 'new');
        },
        onAccessExpired: (_, __) => print('session expired')),
    const DiomanRetry(max: 1),
  ]);
  try {
    dio.interceptors.add(net); // after log → counts true outbound

    print('=== installed: ${api.inspect().map((p) => p.$2).join(' → ')} ===\n');

    // ── 1. Cache: second identical GET is served from the store ───────────────
    print('[1] DiomanCache hit');
    final c1 = await api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/1',
        call: DiomanCall(
            options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true}));
    final before = net.count;
    final c2 = await api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/1',
        call: DiomanCall(
            options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true}));
    print('  1st: ${c1.status} ${c1.message}  data=${c1.data}');
    print('  2nd: ${c2.status} ${c2.message}');
    _check(net.count - before == 0,
        'second identical GET served from cache (0 net calls)');
    print('');

    // ── 2. Share: N concurrent same-key GETs collapse to ONE network call ─────
    print('[2] DiomanShare dedup');
    final n0 = net.count;
    final shared = await Future.wait([
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/5',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/5',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/5',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
    ]);
    final sharedCalls = net.count - n0;
    final allEqual =
        shared.every((r) => r.data.toString() == shared.first.data.toString());
    print(
        '  3 concurrent callers, net calls added: $sharedCalls, all equal: $allEqual');
    _check(sharedCalls == 1,
        '3 concurrent same-key GETs collapse to 1 network call');
    _check(allEqual, 'every caller receives the same shared result');
    print('');

    // ── 3. Auth proactive refresh: concurrent expired-token requests refresh
    //       exactly once, then all succeed ──────────────────────────────────────
    print('[3] DiomanAuth proactive refresh (token starts expired)');
    final r0 = net.count;
    await Future.wait([
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/6',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/7',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/8',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
    ]);
    print('  refreshCount=$refreshCount, token now=${tm.accessToken}, '
        'net calls added: ${net.count - r0}');
    _check(refreshCount == 1,
        'concurrent expiring requests collapse to exactly ONE refresh');
    _check(
        net.count - r0 == 3, 'all 3 requests still went out over the network');
    print('');

    // ── 4. DiomanRepathOptions: default substitutes {id}; enabled:false leaves
    //       the literal placeholder in the path (→ 404) ───────────────────────
    print('[4] DiomanRepathOptions');
    final repathed =
        await api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/{id}', query: {'id': 9});
    _check(
        repathed.data['id'] == 9, 'default repath substitutes {id} → /todos/9');
    try {
      await api.send<dynamic>(
        'GET',
        '/todos/{id}',
        query: {'id': 9},
        call: DiomanCall(options: {DiomanRepath.option: false}),
      );
      _check(false,
          'DiomanRepathOptions(enabled:false) should have left a literal, invalid path');
    } on DiomanResponse catch (e) {
      _check(e.status == 404,
          'DiomanRepathOptions(enabled:false) leaves the literal placeholder → 404');
    }
    print('');

    // ── 5. DiomanCacheOptions(enabled:false): bypasses the cache hit ───────────
    print('[5] DiomanCacheOptions(enabled: false)');
    await api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/1',
        call: DiomanCall(options: {
          DiomanCache.option: true,
          DiomanShare.option: true
        })); // still a cache hit from step 1
    final beforeBypass = net.count;
    await api.send<dynamic>(
      'GET',
      '/todos/1',
      call: DiomanCall(options: {DiomanCache.option: false}),
    );
    _check(net.count - beforeBypass == 1,
        'DiomanCacheOptions(enabled:false) bypasses an otherwise-cached hit');
    print('');

    // ── 6. DiomanShareOptions(enabled:false): opts one caller out of dedup ─────
    print('[6] DiomanShareOptions(enabled: false)');
    final n1 = net.count;
    await Future.wait([
      api.send<dynamic>('GET', '/todos/10',
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true})),
      api.send<dynamic>(
        'GET',
        '/todos/10',
        call: DiomanCall(options: {DiomanShare.option: false}),
      ),
    ]);
    _check(net.count - n1 == 2,
        'the opted-out caller issues its own request instead of joining the shared one');
    print('');

    // ── 7. DiomanKeyOptions: a custom key makes two DIFFERENT endpoints share
    //       one cache entry ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    print('[7] DiomanKeyOptions(key: ...)');
    const sharedKey = 'dioman-real-run-shared-key';
    final k1 = await api.send<dynamic>(
      'GET',
      '/todos/11',
      call: DiomanCall(key: sharedKey, options: {DiomanCache.option: true}),
    );
    final beforeKeyed = net.count;
    final k2 = await api.send<dynamic>(
      'GET',
      '/todos/12', // a DIFFERENT path, but same forced key → same cache entry
      call: DiomanCall(key: sharedKey, options: {DiomanCache.option: true}),
    );
    _check(net.count - beforeKeyed == 0,
        'a forced shared key makes a different path hit the same cache entry');
    _check(k2.data['id'] == k1.data['id'],
        'the second call returns the FIRST call\'s cached payload (id ${k1.data['id']})');
    print('');

    // ── 8. DiomanRetryOptions(shouldRetry: ...): forces a retry on a 404, which
    //       the plugin default (408/429/500/502/503/504) would never retry ──────
    print('[8] DiomanRetryOptions(shouldRetry: ...)');
    final beforeRetry = net.count;
    try {
      await api.send<dynamic>(
        'GET',
        '/todos/999999', // jsonplaceholder 404s this
        call: DiomanCall(options: {
          DiomanRetry.option: DiomanRetryOptions(shouldRetry: (_, __) => true)
        }),
      );
    } on DiomanResponse catch (_) {
      // Expected — jsonplaceholder genuinely 404s; we only care how many times.
    }
    _check(net.count - beforeRetry == 2,
        'shouldRetry:true drives max(1)+1=2 attempts on a 404 the default shouldRetry ignores');
    print('');

    // debug 现在属于客户端配置。
    final captured = <String>[];
    final debugApi =
        Dioman(dio: Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: _base)), debug: captured.add);
    try {
      await debugApi.get<dynamic>('/todos/13');
      _check(captured.isNotEmpty, 'debug 收到日志');
    } finally {
      debugApi.dispose();
      debugApi.dio.close(force: true);
    }

    // ── 10. DiomanAuthOptions(enabled:false): request proceeds unprotected ────
    print('[10] DiomanAuthOptions(enabled: false)');
    final authRes = await api.send<dynamic>(
      'GET',
      '/todos/14',
      call: DiomanCall(options: {DiomanAuth.option: false}),
    );
    _check(authRes.status == 200,
        'DiomanAuthOptions(enabled:false) still lets the request through');
    print('');

    _check(api.pending == 0, '所有逻辑请求释放');
    print('=== ALL CHECKS PASSED ===');
  } finally {
    api.cancelAll();
    api.dispose();
    dio.close(force: true);
  }
}

import '../client.dart';
import '../context.dart';
import '../options.dart';
import '../plugin.dart';
import '../request.dart';
import '../response.dart';
import '../stages.dart';

/// 宿主上报的网络档位；插件不探测网络，也不负责离线排队。
enum NetworkQuality {
  /// 强连接。
  excellent,

  /// 普通移动网络。
  good,

  /// 弱连接。
  poor,

  /// 无连接，仍作为普通配置档位处理。
  none,
}

/// 某档的超时；null 保留请求已有值。
class DiomanTimeouts {
  /// [connect]、[receive]、[send] 分别覆盖三个 Dio 超时，例如 `DiomanTimeouts(receive: Duration(seconds: 2))`。
  const DiomanTimeouts({this.connect, this.receive, this.send});

  /// 连接超时。
  final Duration? connect;

  /// 接收超时。
  final Duration? receive;

  /// 发送超时。
  final Duration? send;
}

/// 请求级超时配置，按档位取并集，单档整体替换。
class DiomanTimeoutOptions {
  /// [enabled] 为 false 时保留原配置，[timeouts] 只覆盖列出的档位。
  const DiomanTimeoutOptions({this.enabled, this.timeouts});

  /// 本次开关。
  final bool? enabled;

  /// 本次覆盖的档位。
  final Map<NetworkQuality, DiomanTimeouts>? timeouts;
}

/// 通过 next(patch) 写超时字段，实际计时交给 Dio。
class DiomanTimeout<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> extends DiomanPlugin<R> {
  /// [probe] 每请求同步求值一次，例如 `DiomanTimeout(probe: () => NetworkQuality.good)`。
  const DiomanTimeout(
      {required this.probe,
      this.timeouts = defaultTimeouts,
      this.enabled = true});

  /// 当前档位提供器，抛错按 bug 原样落定。
  final NetworkQuality Function() probe;

  /// 档位配置。
  final Map<NetworkQuality, DiomanTimeouts> timeouts;

  /// 安装期开关。
  @override
  final bool enabled;

  /// 稳定名称。
  static const pluginName = 'dioman:timeout';

  /// 请求级 bool 或 DiomanTimeoutOptions 键。
  static final option = DiomanOptionKey<DiomanTimeoutOptions>(pluginName);

  /// excellent 与 none 的默认连接时长。
  static const defaultConnect = Duration(seconds: 10);

  /// 旧版默认值：仅写连接超时。
  static const defaultTimeouts = {
    NetworkQuality.excellent: DiomanTimeouts(connect: defaultConnect),
    NetworkQuality.good: DiomanTimeouts(connect: Duration(seconds: 15)),
    NetworkQuality.poor: DiomanTimeouts(connect: Duration(seconds: 30)),
    NetworkQuality.none: DiomanTimeouts(connect: defaultConnect),
  };

  /// 返回稳定名称。
  @override
  String get name => pluginName;

  /// 请求整形阶段。
  @override
  DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.prepare;

  /// 复制配置映射，实例不共享可变状态。
  @override
  DiomanPluginInstance<R> setup(Dioman<R> client) =>
      _Timeout<R>(probe, DiomanTimeoutOptions(timeouts: Map.of(timeouts)));
}

/// 执行器只观察公开结果，缺能力在发请求前被识别。
void main() {
  test('[client.classify] 归类只读取 kind reason status，原始数据不参与判断', () {
    final request = DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(path: '/classification'));
    for (final entry in {
      DiomanKind.biz: 'business:503', DiomanKind.http: 'http:503',
      DiomanKind.cancel: 'canceled', DiomanKind.timeout: 'timeout',
      DiomanKind.network: 'network', DiomanKind.parse: 'parse',
      DiomanKind.breaker: 'breaker_open', DiomanKind.offline: 'offline',
      DiomanKind.auth: 'denied',
    }.entries) {
      final response = DiomanResponse<Object?>(ok: false, kind: entry.key,
          status: 503, httpStatus: 200, request: request,
          reason: 'denied', data: {'code': 0});
      expect(classify(response), entry.value);
    }
  });
  test('[common.missingCapabilities] mock 在链内，raw-business-code 未声明', () {
    expect(supportedCapabilities, contains('mock-in-chain'));
    expect(missingCapabilities({'requires': ['mock-in-chain', 'raw-business-code']}),
        ['raw-business-code']);
  });
  test('[ScenarioRun.execute] 缺能力和未声明维度分别跳过，且不访问服务', () async {
    for (final missing in [true, false]) {
      final scenario = {'id': 'runner-check', 'requires': missing ? ['raw-business-code'] : [],
        'networks': ['normal'], 'styles': ['http'], 'expect': <String, Object?>{}};
      final run = ScenarioRun(scenario, missing ? 'normal' : 'weak', 'http', 'runner-check');
      final result = await run.execute();
      final reasons = result['reasons'] as List;
      expect(reasons.single, startsWith(missing ? 'skipped: missing ' : '不适用：'));
    }
  });
}

/// 封装 ApiEvidence 测试夹具。
/// 公开 API 探针的可追溯证据，只有断言成立才落盘。
class ApiEvidence {
  /// 声明到运行证据的映射。
  final hits = <String, Set<String>>{};

  /// 本次真实服务轮次。
  final run = 'dioman-api-${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}';

  /// 已完成真实请求的证据。
  final observations = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];

  /// mark 的测试实现。
  /// 记录已实际调用或读取的公开符号。
  void mark(Iterable<String> names, String context) {
    for (final name in names) {
      (hits[name] ??= {}).add('$run:$context');
    }
  }

  /// read 的测试实现。
  /// 读取静态字段时保留精确的符号证据。
  void read(String name, Object? value) => mark([name], tkPropertyRead);

  /// constructed 的测试实现。
  /// 为显式传入的构造参数登记证据。
  T constructed<T>(String type, T value, List<String> args) {
    mark([type, '$type.new', for (final a in args) '$type.new($a)'],
        'construct');
    return value;
  }

  /// check 的测试实现。
  /// 校验执行事实，不允许软断言。
  void check(bool valid, String description) {
    if (!valid) throw StateError(description);
  }
}

/// 使用新客户端执行真实请求，只为实际构造和调用登记证据。
Future<ApiEvidence> runApiProbes() async {
  final evidence = ApiEvidence();
  final client = ScenarioClient({
    'client': {'plugins': {}}
  }, evidence.run, serverUrl, tkHttp);
  try {
    final result = await client.api.send<dynamic>('GET', tkTPing,
        call: DiomanCall(headers: client.headers));
    evidence.check(result.ok, '真实 ping 成功');
    evidence.check(client.api.pending == 0, '逻辑请求释放');
    evidence.mark([
      'Dioman',
      'Dioman.new',
      'Dioman.new(dio)',
      'Dioman.send',
      'Dioman.pending',
      'DiomanResponse',
      'DiomanResponse.kind'
    ], '真实 ping');
    evidence.observations
        .add({'kind': result.kind.name, 'status': result.status});
    inspectApiFields(
        evidence, {'Dioman': client.api, 'DiomanResponse': result});
  } finally {
    await client.dispose();
  }
  evidence.mark(['Dioman.dispose'], tkTeardown);
  final target = File(tkAcceptResultsApiEvidenceJson);
  target.parent.createSync(recursive: true);
  target.writeAsStringSync(const JsonEncoder.withIndent(tkJsonIndent).convert({
    tkRunId: evidence.run,
    tkHits: {
      for (final entry in evidence.hits.entries) entry.key: entry.value.toList()
    },
    tkObservations: evidence.observations,
  }));
  return evidence;
}

/// 读取已经实际构造的实例字段；不虚报未构造类型。
Future<void> writeApiEvidence(
        ApiEvidence evidence, Map<String, Object> values) async =>
    inspectApiFields(evidence, values);

/// 由台账生成的逐字段读取入口。
void inspectApiFields(ApiEvidence evidence, Map<String, Object> values) =>
    generatedInspect(evidence, values);

"""旧版一轮基准：python tool/bench_legacy.py --out <结果.json>。

B6 交错 A/B：本命令与 dart run benchmark/pipeline.dart 轮流各跑三轮。
只从固定提交 895d959 运行；临时 worktree 位于本工作区内，finally 移除。

"""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REVISION = '895d959'


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    temporary = ROOT / ('.bench-legacy-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
    dart = shutil.which('dart')
    if not dart:
        raise RuntimeError('dart unavailable')
    added = False
    try:
        subprocess.run(['git', 'worktree', 'add', '--detach', str(temporary), REVISION],
                       cwd=ROOT, check=True, stdout=sys.stderr)
        added = True
        package = temporary / 'dioman'
        subprocess.run([dart, 'pub', 'get', '--offline'], cwd=package,
                       check=True, stdout=sys.stderr)
        result = subprocess.run([dart, 'run', 'benchmark/legacy.dart'],
                                cwd=package, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        sys.stderr.write(result.stderr)
        data = json.loads(result.stdout.strip())
        data['implementation'] = 'legacy-' + REVISION
        output = json.dumps(data, indent=2) + '\n'
        if args.out:
            args.out.write_text(output, encoding='utf-8')
        else:
            sys.stdout.write(output)
    finally:
        if added:
            assert temporary.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT) and temporary.name.startswith('.bench-legacy-')
            subprocess.run(['git', 'worktree', 'remove', '--force', str(temporary)],
                           cwd=ROOT, check=True, stdout=sys.stderr)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

/// 按 CONTRACT §7 的 32 位线性同余算法生成 [size] 个字节。
///
/// `state = state * 1664525 + 1013904223 mod 2^32`，每步输出高 8 位；
/// 与其他语言逐字节一致，不依赖任何库。
///
/// - [seed] 起始状态，无符号 32 位（0..4294967295）。
/// - [size] 字节数，非负。
///
/// 返回长度为 [size] 的字节数组。
///
/// ```dart
/// seedBytes(1, 4); // 3c 5e 81 b4
/// ```
Uint8List seedBytes(int seed, int size) {
  if (seed < 0 || seed > 0xFFFFFFFF) {
    throw RangeError.range(seed, 0, 0xFFFFFFFF);
  }
  final out = Uint8List(size);
  var state = seed;
  for (var i = 0; i < size; i++) {
    state = (state * 1664525 + 1013904223) & 0xFFFFFFFF;
    out[i] = state >> 24;
  }
  return out;
}

/// 场景请求体是否为 multipart（只含单个键 `multipart` 的对象）。
bool isMultipartBody(dynamic body) =>
    body is Map && body.length == 1 && body.containsKey(tkMultipart);

/// 把场景里的 multipart 描述构造成 dio 的 [FormData]。
///
/// 先按对象顺序写 `fields`，再按数组顺序写 `files`；文件内容由 `seed`/`size`
/// 经 [seedBytes] 生成，`Content-Type` 固定 `application/octet-stream`。
/// 文件用 [MultipartFile.fromBytes]，可被 FormData 克隆，供重放与内部重试重建。
///
/// - [spec] 即 `request.body.multipart`：`{fields?, files?}`。
///
/// 返回新的 [FormData]。
FormData buildMultipart(Map<String, dynamic> spec) {
  final form = FormData();
  for (final e in obj(spec['fields']).entries) {
    form.fields.add(MapEntry(e.key, '${e.value}'));
  }
  for (final raw in spec['files'] as List? ?? const []) {
    final f = obj(raw);
    form.files.add(MapEntry(
        f['name'] as String,
        MultipartFile.fromBytes(seedBytes(f['seed'] as int, f['size'] as int),
            filename: f['filename'] as String,
            contentType: DioMediaType('application', 'octet-stream'))));
  }
  return form;
}

/// 把场景 `request.body` 转成发给 dio 的请求体：multipart 构造 [FormData]，其余原样。
dynamic requestBody(dynamic body) => isMultipartBody(body)
    ? buildMultipart(obj((body as Map)[tkMultipart]))
    : body;

/// 服务端 `read_delay_ms` 控制参数的字段名。
const readDelayKey = 'read_delay_ms';

/// 递归找出场景里最大的 `read_delay_ms`（毫秒），没有则返回 0。
///
/// 服务端在读请求体前停顿期间不轮询 body，客户端提前超时关闭连接后，处理器仍会
/// 在途到停顿结束；收尾等待 inflight 归零时要把这段时间算进去。
///
/// - [node] 场景 JSON 的任意节点（Map / List / 标量）。
int maxReadDelayMs(dynamic node) {
  var best = 0;
  if (node is Map) {
    for (final e in node.entries) {
      final v = e.value;
      final here = e.key == readDelayKey && v is num ? v.toInt() : 0;
      final deeper = maxReadDelayMs(v);
      best = [best, here, deeper].reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b);
    }
  } else if (node is List) {
    for (final x in node) {
      final deeper = maxReadDelayMs(x);
      if (deeper > best) best = deeper;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/// 按点分路径（如 `meta.conn`）从 [data] 取值，路径不存在返回 null。
dynamic dottedField(dynamic data, String path) {
  dynamic cur = data;
  for (final part in path.split('.')) {
    if (cur is! Map || !cur.containsKey(part)) return null;
    cur = cur[part];
  }
  return cur;
}

/// 判定结果项的 `same_as` 断言：两个请求都成功，且 data 的同一字段相等。
///
/// - [id] 当前结果项 id。
/// - [records] 全部请求事实，按 id 索引。
/// - [spec] 即 `same_as: {id, field}`。
///
/// 通过返回 null，否则返回失败原因。
String? checkSameAs(
    String id, Map<String, dynamic> records, Map<String, dynamic> spec) {
  final otherId = '${spec[tkId]}';
  final field = '${spec['field']}';
  final mine = obj(records[id]);
  final other = obj(records[otherId]);
  if (other.isEmpty) return '$id: same_as 引用的 $otherId 缺少请求结果';
  if (mine[tkOk] != true || other[tkOk] != true) {
    return '$id: same_as 要求双方都成功（ok=true），实际 $id=${mine[tkOk]}、$otherId=${other[tkOk]}';
  }
  final a = dottedField(mine[tkData], field);
  final b = dottedField(other[tkData], field);
  if (a == null || b == null) return '$id: same_as 字段 $field 缺失（$a / $b）';
  if (a != b) return '$id: same_as $field 不等，$id=$a、$otherId=$b';
  return null;
}

/// obj 的测试辅助入口。
/// 统一 JSON 对象转换。
Map<String, dynamic> obj(dynamic v) =>
    v is Map ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(v) : {};

/// ms 的测试辅助入口。
/// 毫秒转换。
Duration ms(num n) => Duration(milliseconds: n.toInt());

/// pause 的测试辅助入口。
/// 等待短时间。
Future<void> pause(num n) => Future<void>.delayed(ms(n));

/// ioClient 的测试辅助入口。
/// 显式使用 io adapter，不读取系统代理。
Dio ioClient(String base, {int budget = defaultBudget}) {
  final d = Dio(BaseOptions(
      baseUrl: base,
      connectTimeout: ms(budget),
      receiveTimeout: ms(budget),
      sendTimeout: ms(budget)));
  d.httpClientAdapter =
      ServiceGuardAdapter(IOHttpClientAdapter(createHttpClient: () {
    return HttpClient()..findProxy = (_) => 'DIRECT';
  }

  final all =
      Future.wait([fire('/slow/a', 1), fire('/slow/b', 2), fire('/slow/c', 3)]);

  await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 150));
  emit('cancelAll() — simulating page navigation', _Kind.system);
  api.cancelAll('left page');

  await all;
  emit('cancelled: $aborted · completed: $done', _Kind.system);
}

// 10. loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Future<void> _runLoading(_Emit emit) async {
  int counter = 0;

  final mock = _FakeServer()
    ..add('GET:/task/a', (_) async {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
      return _json('{"task":"a"}');
    })
    ..add('GET:/task/b', (_) async {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 320));
      return _json('{"task":"b"}');
    })
    ..add('GET:/task/c', (_) async {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 260));
      return _json('{"task":"c"}');
    });

  final dio = Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: 'https://api.demo.dev'));
  dio.httpClientAdapter = mock;
  final api = Dioman(dio: dio, plugins: [
    DiomanLoading(onChanged: (on) {
      counter += on ? 1 : -1;
      emit('counter = $counter  (${on ? "+1" : "−1"})', _Kind.system);
    }),
    _logPlugin(emit),
  ]);

  emit('fire 3 concurrent requests (/task/a · /task/b · /task/c)…',
      _Kind.system);
  await Future.wait([
    api.send<dynamic>('GET', call: _sourceCall, '/task/a'),
    api.send<dynamic>('GET', call: _sourceCall, '/task/b'),
    api.send<dynamic>('GET', call: _sourceCall, '/task/c'),
  ]);
  emit('all done  counter = $counter', _Kind.response);
}

          type: DioExceptionType.badResponse,
        );

      return _json('{"id":1,"name":"Alice","role":"admin"}');
    });

  dio.httpClientAdapter = mock;

    DiomanAuth(
        tokenManager: InMemoryTokenManager(access: 'demo-access'),
        onRefresh: (tokens, _) => tokens.setTokens(accessToken: 'new-access'),
        onAccessExpired: (_, __) {}),
    _logPlugin(emit)
  ]);

  emit('GET /profile  (token will expire → refresh → replay)', _Kind.system);

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:dioman/dioman.dart';

import 'scenarios.dart';

/// 基线请求的绝对地址，不产生真实网络访问。
const _baseUrl = 'https://benchmark.invalid';

/// 所有场景共用的路径。
const _path = '/value';

/// 立即返回的成功信封；每次 fetch 新建响应，Dio 每次解码出新 Map。
const _envelope = '{"code":0,"data":{"value":1},"message":"ok"}';

/// 薄假传输，只计数并返回新信封，没有路由表或定时器。
class _ImmediateAdapter implements HttpClientAdapter {
  /// 记录实际传输调用数。
  int sends = 0;

  /// 立即创建独立响应体，交给真实 Dio Transformer 解码。
  @override
  Future<ResponseBody> fetch(RequestOptions options,
      Stream<Uint8List>? requestStream, Future<void>? cancelFuture) async {
    sends++;
    return ResponseBody.fromString(_envelope, 200, headers: {
      Headers.contentTypeHeader: [Headers.jsonContentType]
    });
  }

  /// 假传输没有需要释放的连接。
  @override
  void close({bool force = false}) {}
}

/// 有效凭证，基准不触发刷新。
class _Tokens implements DiomanTokenManager {
  @override
  String get accessToken => 'benchmark-token';
  @override
  String get refreshToken => 'benchmark-refresh';
  @override
  void setTokens({required String accessToken, String? refreshToken}) {}
  @override
  void clear() {}
}

/// 新客户端六场景，与固定旧提交共享 runner 和发送数自检。
class _PipelineClient implements DiomanBenchClient {
  _PipelineClient(this.scenario) {
    dio.httpClientAdapter = adapter;
    final full = scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s3 ||
        scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s4;
    final shared = scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s5;
    api = Dioman(dio: dio, plugins: [
      if (full) ...[
        const DiomanEnvs([]),
        DiomanTimeout(probe: () => NetworkQuality.excellent),
        const DiomanRepath(),
        const DiomanFilter(),
        const DiomanCache(expires: Duration(hours: 1)),
        DiomanOffline(
            isOnline: () => true, onConnectivityChanged: connectivity.stream),
        DiomanLoading(onChanged: (_) {}),
        DiomanAuth(
            tokenManager: _Tokens(),
            onRefresh: (_, __) {},
            onAccessExpired: (_, __) {}),
        const DiomanBreaker(),
        DiomanNotify(notify: (_, __) {}),
        // 空路由代表不命中；不额外发 mock 探测请求。
        const DiomanMock(),
      ],
      if (full || shared) const DiomanShare(policy: DiomanSharePolicy.start),
      if (full || scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s6) const DiomanRetry(max: 2),
    ]);
  }
  final DiomanBenchScenario scenario;
  final dio = Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: _baseUrl));
  final adapter = _ImmediateAdapter();
  final connectivity = StreamController<bool>.broadcast();
  late final Dioman api;
  int sequence = 0;
  @override
  int get sends => adapter.sends;
  @override
  Future<void> operation() async {
    if (scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s1) {
      await dio.get<dynamic>(_path);
    } else if (scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s5) {
      await Future.wait(List.generate(
          diomanBenchConcurrency,
          (_) => api.get<dynamic>(_path,
              call: DiomanCall(options: {DiomanShare.option: true}))));
    } else if (scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s6) {
      await api.post<dynamic>(_path, data: {
        'items': [
          {'value': 1}
        ]
      });
    } else {
      await api.get<dynamic>(_path,
          query: scenario == DiomanBenchScenario.s3
              ? {'sequence': sequence++}
              : null,
          call: DiomanCall(
              options: {DiomanCache.option: true, DiomanShare.option: true}));
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> dispose() async {
    api.dispose();
    dio.close(force: true);
    await connectivity.close();
  }
}

/// createProbeProxy 的测试辅助入口。
/// 为独立探针创建代理并立即持久化归属，便于中断后安全清理。
Future<Map<String, dynamic>> createProbeProxy(Dio control, String run) async {
  File(tkAcceptResultsProxiesNdjson).parent.createSync(recursive: true);
  final proxy = obj((await control.post<dynamic>(tkProxies,
          data: {tkUpstream: upstream, tkProfile: tkNormal}))
      .data);
  File(tkAcceptResultsProxiesNdjson).writeAsStringSync(
      '${jsonEncode({
            tkRunId: run,
            tkCreated: proxy,
            tkControl: netemUrl,
            tkUpstream: upstream
          })}\n',
      mode: FileMode.append);
  return proxy;
}

/// deleteProbeProxy 的测试辅助入口。
/// 只删除调用方本次创建的代理，并登记删除成功。
Future<void> deleteProbeProxy(
    Dio control, Map<String, dynamic> proxy, String run) async {
  await control.delete<dynamic>('/proxies/${proxy[tkId]}');
  File(tkAcceptResultsProxiesNdjson).writeAsStringSync(
      '${jsonEncode({tkRunId: run, tkDeleted: proxy[tkId]})}\n',
      mode: FileMode.append);
}

/// 服务器限流或熔断时立即中断，禁止领取后续请求。
bool serviceBlocked = false;

/// 服务端用来要求停止的状态码：限流与熔断。
const guardedStatuses = {429, 503};

/// 响应体是否带 testkit 的注入标记（`"reason":"injected"` / `"injected failure"`）。
///
/// testkit 对场景脚本主动注入的失败会在 data 里写 `reason: injected`，真实的
/// 限流/CPU 熔断不会带这个标记。[body] 为原始响应字节；返回是否为注入响应。
///
/// ```dart
/// isInjectedResponse(utf8.encode('{"data":{"reason":"injected"}}')); // true
/// ```
bool isInjectedResponse(List<int> body) =>
    RegExp(r'"reason"\s*:\s*"injected(?: failure)?"')
        .hasMatch(utf8.decode(body, allowMalformed: true));

/// 已解析的响应数据是否带 testkit 的注入标记（`data.reason` 以 `injected` 开头）。
///
/// business 风格下注入的 429/503 以 HTTP 200 加业务码返回，到不了传输层的字节
/// 检查；拦截器和转换器拿到的是解析后的 [data]，用它判定。
bool isInjectedData(dynamic data) {
  final reason = obj(obj(data)[tkData])['reason'];
  return reason is String && reason.startsWith('injected');
}

/// checkServiceStatus 的测试辅助入口。
/// 标记服务端要求停止的响应，不把它交给自动重试。
///
/// [data] 为已解析的响应数据；带注入标记的 429/503 是 testkit 主动注入的，直接放行。
void checkServiceStatus(int? status, {dynamic data}) {
  if (guardedStatuses.contains(status) && !isInjectedData(data)) {
    serviceBlocked = true;
    throw StateError('服务返回 $status，按用户要求停止验收，未重启服务');
  }
}

/// 封装 ServiceGuardAdapter 测试夹具。
/// 在插件重放共用的传输层阻断限流及熔断响应。
class ServiceGuardAdapter implements HttpClientAdapter {
  /// 构造独立测试实例。
  /// 包装真实 io 传输，不合成成功响应。
  ServiceGuardAdapter(this.inner);

  /// 被包装的真实传输层。
  final HttpClientAdapter inner;

  /// fetch 的测试实现。
  /// 在进入重试链之前检测服务状态。
  @override
  Future<ResponseBody> fetch(RequestOptions options, Stream<Uint8List>? stream,
      Future<void>? cancelFuture) async {
    final response = await inner.fetch(options, stream, cancelFuture);
    if (!guardedStatuses.contains(response.statusCode)) return response;
    // 429/503 才读响应体：场景脚本主动注入的放行，真实限流/熔断照旧整轮停止。
    final body = await response.stream
        .fold<List<int>>(<int>[], (all, chunk) => all..addAll(chunk));
    if (!isInjectedResponse(body)) {
      try {
        checkServiceStatus(response.statusCode);
      } catch (error) {
        throw DioException(
            requestOptions: options,
            type: DioExceptionType.cancel,
            error: error);
      }
    }
    return ResponseBody(
        Stream.value(Uint8List.fromList(body)), response.statusCode,
        headers: response.headers,
        statusMessage: response.statusMessage,
        isRedirect: response.isRedirect,
        redirects: response.redirects);
  }

  /// close 的测试实现。
  /// 释放自己拥有的传输层。
  @override
  void close({bool force = false}) => inner.close(force: force);
}

const refreshPath = '/t/auth/refresh';

/// 登录接口。
const loginPath = '/t/auth/login';

codex
Analysis and all 5,010 local tests pass, but the acceptance migration drops service-stop protection and total-budget enforcement. The real-network example also fails on an expected response because it uses the non-throwing API.

Full review comments:

- [P2] Preserve the service guard for business-style responses — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\test\accept\lib\common.dart:377-377
  When the service returns HTTP 200 with a non-injected business code of 429 or 503, removing the response interceptor leaves no guard that sets `serviceBlocked`: `ServiceGuardAdapter` only checks HTTP status codes. These responses can now trigger automatic retries and subsequent scenarios instead of stopping acceptance. Restore an equivalent logical-status check before retry processing.

- [P2] Retain or explicitly reject total request budgets — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\test\accept\lib\runner.dart:197-201
  For scenarios configuring `client.plugins.timeout.total_ms`, the removed budget timer was the only implementation of the total deadline. The replacement only maps connect/send/receive timeouts, while `pluginFields` still accepts `total_ms`. Such scenarios now silently run without their configured budget, especially across retries or offline waits. Preserve the host deadline or explicitly identify this configuration as unsupported.

- [P2] Assert the returned failure instead of expecting send to throw — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\example\real_run.dart:171-175
  On the expected 404, `api.send` returns a failed `DiomanResponse` rather than throwing it. Execution therefore always reaches `_check(false, ...)`, whose `StateError` bypasses this catch and terminates the real-network example before the remaining checks. Inspect the returned response, or use the throwing `request` API. The migrated Flutter example's business-error and cancellation catches have the same mismatch.
